\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\,\left(i^{k}\,e^{\pm m_{d-2}\varphi}\prod_{j=1}^{d-2}\sin^{m_{j}}(\theta_{j})G_{m_{j-1}-m_{j}}^{m_{j}+\frac{d-2}{2}-\frac{j-1}{2}}\left(\cos(\theta_{j})\right)\right)

where
k=0,1
, and
0\leq m_{d-2}\leq\cdots\leq m_{1}\leq m_{0}\leq n
, while

(\theta_{1},\cdots,\theta_{d-2},\varphi)\in[0,\pi]^{d-2}\times[0,2\pi]
are the usual spherical coordinates on the sphere, and
G_{\alpha}^{\beta}
are the Gegenbauer polynomials or ultra-spherical polynomials of degree
\alpha
and order
\beta
. Thanks to the recurrence relation

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dx}G_{\alpha}^{\beta}=2\beta\,C_{\alpha-1}^{\beta+1},

and using the elementary estimate
|Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)|\leq N_{d}(n)
, we deduce that for all
l\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists a universal constant
\Gamma_{d}(l)<\infty
such that

\displaystyle|\nabla_{\omega}^{l}Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)|\leq\Gamma_{d}(l)n^{l}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}.

(3.43)

In particular, we deduce that

\displaystyle|\nabla u|\leq|\partial_{r}u|+\frac{1}{r}|\nabla_{\omega}u|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n\,a_{n,k}\,r^{n-1}-(n+d-2)b_{n,k}\,r^{-(n+d-1)}\right)Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}\left(a_{n,k}\,r^{n-1}+b_{n,k}\,r^{-(n+d-1)}\right)\nabla_{\omega}Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\,\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|r^{n-1}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\Big(n+d-2+\Gamma_{d}(1)n\Big)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d-1)}

\displaystyle=\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|r^{n-1}+\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d-1)}.

(3.44)

Therefore, we have by (
3.38
) and (
3.39
)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq 4\sqrt{\beta(d)}\left(1+\Gamma_{1}(d)\right)\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha b\right)^{n-1+\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n+d-2\right)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2n+d-2}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}-1}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\sqrt{\beta(d)}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\left(\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}nN_{d}(n)\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+4\sqrt{\beta(d)}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{4(n+d-1)^{2}}{(2n+d-2)^{2}}\left(n+d-2\right)\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{d}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\left(\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

where we used Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, and the identity (
3.16
)

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx=\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right)

which implies the sharp inequality

\displaystyle\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx.

∎

Now, we get an estimate on the Hessian matrix of harmonic maps in annular regions.

Theorem 3.10
.

Let
d\geq 3
. Then, there exists a universal constant
C_{d}^{\prime}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, define
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and for all
\mathbf{d\geq 7}
, assume that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right).

(3.45)

For all
0<\alpha<1
, define
\Omega_{\alpha}=B_{\alpha\,b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\alpha^{-1}a}(0)
, and let
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
be a harmonic function. Then, for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{C_{d}^{\prime\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{d-1}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

(3.46)

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\nabla^{2}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{1}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}a_{n,k}r^{n}Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)\right)=\nabla^{2}\left(a_{0,1}+\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{1,k}x_{k}\right)=0.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla^{2}u|\leq|\partial_{r}^{2}u|+\frac{1}{r}|\nabla_{\omega}\left(\partial_{r}u\right)|+\left|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right)\right|+\frac{1}{r}\left|\nabla_{\omega}(\partial_{r}u)\right|+\frac{1}{r^{2}}|\nabla^{2}_{\omega}u|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n(n-1)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|r^{n-2}+\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d)}

\displaystyle+\Gamma_{d}(1)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n(2n-1)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|r^{n-2}+\Gamma_{d}(1)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n(2n+2d-3)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d)}

\displaystyle+\Gamma_{d}(2)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|r^{n-2}+\Gamma_{d}(2)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|r^{n-2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d)}.

(3.47)

Therefore, we get for all
0<\alpha<1

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha\,b\right)^{n-2+\frac{d}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}.

Now, we need to estimate

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d^{2}-7d+12)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}

\displaystyle\geq\left(2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d^{2}-7d+12)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d^{2}-7d+12)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right).

For
d=3
, we get

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)=8n^{3}-n(2n-1)

\displaystyle=8n^{3}-2n^{2}+n\geq 6n^{3}+n.

For
d=4
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)=8n^{3}+8n^{2}-4-4n^{2}

\displaystyle=8n^{3}+4n^{2}-4\geq 8n^{3}.

For
d=5
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)

\displaystyle=8n^{3}+16n^{2}+4n-12-6n^{2}-3n=8n^{3}+10n^{2}+n-12\geq 7n^{3}.

Finally, for
d=6
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)

\displaystyle=8n^{3}+24n^{2}+12n-24-8n^{2}-8n=8n^{3}+16n^{2}+4n-24\geq 4n^{3}.

However, for
d=7
, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)

\displaystyle=8n^{3}+32n^{2}+24n-40-10n^{2}+15n=8n^{3}+22n^{2}+9n-40

which is equal to
-1<0
for
n=1
. Therefore, we need to find a condition on the conformal class for
d\geq 7
so that our estimate is non-trivial and capture the cubic growth of the coefficient in (
3.1
). Let
\varepsilon>0
and let us find a condition on the conformal class such that

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\geq 2\varepsilon\,n^{3}

(3.48)

if and only if

\displaystyle\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\leq\frac{2\left((4-\varepsilon)n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)}{(d-2)n(2n+d-2)}.

For simplicity, let

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \alpha&=\frac{4(d-3)}{4-\varepsilon}\\
\beta&=\frac{(d-3)(d-4)}{4-\varepsilon}\\
\gamma&=\frac{d-2}{2}\\
\delta&=\frac{(d-2)(d-3)}{4-\varepsilon},\end{aligned}\right.

and consider the function

\displaystyle f(x)=\frac{x^{3}+\alpha x^{2}+\beta x-\delta}{x(x+\gamma)}.

We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)

\displaystyle=\frac{3x^{2}+2\alpha\,x+\beta}{x(x+\gamma)}-\frac{(2\,x+\gamma)\left(x^{3}+\alpha\,x^{2}+\beta x-\delta\right)}{x^{2}(x+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{x^{4}+2\gamma\,x^{3}+\left(\alpha\gamma-\beta\right)x^{2}+2\delta\,x+\gamma\delta}{x^{2}(x+\gamma)^{2}}>0

provided that
\alpha\gamma-\beta\geq 0
. We have

\displaystyle\alpha\gamma-\beta=\frac{1}{4-\varepsilon}\left(2(d-3)(d-2)-(d-3)(d-4)\right)=\frac{d(d-3)}{4-\varepsilon}\geq 0\qquad\text{for all}\;\,d\geq 3.

In particular,
f
is strictly increasing on
[1,\infty[
, which implies that

\displaystyle\inf_{n\geq 1}\frac{2\left((4-\varepsilon)n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d-3)(d-4)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)}{(d-2)n(2n+d-2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\left(4-\varepsilon+4(d-3)+(d-3)(d-4)-(d-2)(d-3)\right)}{d(d-2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\left(4-\varepsilon+2(d-3)\right)}{d(d-2)}=\frac{2(2(d-1)-\varepsilon)}{d(d-2)}.

Therefore, (
3.48
) is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}\leq\frac{2(2(d-1)-\varepsilon)}{d(d-2)},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d(d-2)}{2(2(d-1)-\varepsilon)}\right).

(3.49)

Numerical estimation show that for
\varepsilon=0
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{d\geq 3}\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d(d-2)}{2(2(d-1)-\varepsilon)}\right)<\frac{1}{10},

which implies that there exists
\varepsilon_{0}>0
such that (
3.49
) is satisfied provided that

\displaystyle\frac{b}{a}\geq e^{\frac{1}{10}}=1.105\cdots

In general, choosing
\varepsilon=2
, we get (without any conditions if
3\leq d\leq 6
)

\displaystyle 2\left(4n^{3}+4(d-3)n^{2}+(d^{2}-7d+12)n-(d-2)(d-3)\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle-(d-2)n(2n+d-4)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\geq 4n^{3},

and finally (
3.1
) becomes

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}\geq\beta(d)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle+\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(n+d-2)}{2(2n+d)}\left(8n^{3}+2(7d-10)n^{2}+(7d^{2}-20d+16)n+(d-2)\left((d-1)^{2}+1\right)\right)

(3.50)

\displaystyle\times|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\beta(d)\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)

\displaystyle+\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d}\right)

(3.51)

and the condition on the conformal class for
d\geq 7
is

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right).

(3.52)

Furthermore, (
3.2
) implies that

\displaystyle\beta(d)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha b\right)^{n-2+\frac{d}{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{\Gamma}_{d}(1)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(2n+d-4)\alpha^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\widetilde{\Gamma}_{d}(1)\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}N_{d}(n)\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}N_{d}(n)\frac{(2n+d)(n+d-1)^{2}(n+d-2)}{(2n^{3}+1)}\alpha^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{d}^{\prime\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{d-1}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

which concludes the proof of the theorem.
∎

Now, we move to inequalities involving various derivatives.

Theorem 3.11
.

Let
d\geq 3
. Then, there exists a universal constant
\Gamma_{d}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, define
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{4}
. For all
0<\alpha<1
, define
\Omega_{\alpha}=B_{\alpha\,b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\alpha^{-1}a}(0)
, and let
u\in W^{1,2}(\Omega)
be a harmonic function. Then, there exists
c\in\mathbb{R}
such that for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have the estimate

\displaystyle\left\|u-c\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

(3.53)

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle u(r,\omega)-a_{0,1}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}a_{n,k}\,r^{n}\,Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}b_{n,k}\,r^{-(n+d-2)}\,Y_{n}^{k}(\omega).

Recall that for all
1<p<\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|f\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}=\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\mathscr{L}^{d}\left(\Omega\cap\left\{x:|f(x)|>t\right\}\right)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}dt.

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{d}\left(\Omega\cap\left\{x:|x|^{n}>t\right\}\right)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\beta(d)\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,t\leq a^{n}\\
&\beta(d)\left(b^{d}-t^{\frac{d}{n}}\right)\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,a^{n}<t<b^{n}\\
&0\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,t\geq b^{n}.\end{aligned}\right.

(3.54)

and that for all
n\geq 1

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}^{d}\left(\Omega\cap\left\{x:\frac{1}{|x|^{n}}>t\right\}\right)=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\beta(d)\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,t\leq\frac{1}{b^{n}}\\
&\beta(d)\left(\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d}{n}}}-a^{d}\right)\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,\frac{1}{b^{n}}<t<\frac{1}{a^{n}}\\
&0\qquad&&\text{for all}\;\,t\geq\frac{1}{a^{n}}\end{aligned}\right.,

(3.55)

Therefore, for
p=\dfrac{2d}{d-2}>2
, we have

\displaystyle\left\||x|^{n}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle=\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}a^{n}\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\int_{a^{n}}^{b^{n}}\left(b^{d}-t^{\frac{d}{n}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}dt\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}a^{n}\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}(b^{n}-a^{n})b^{\frac{d}{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}b^{n+\frac{d}{p}}=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}b^{n+\frac{d-2}{2}}.

Notice that
p>2

Likewise, we get for all
n\geq 1

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|x|^{n}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}=\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\frac{1}{b^{n}}\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\int_{\frac{1}{b^{n}}}^{\frac{1}{a^{n}}}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d}{n}}}-a^{d}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}dt\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\frac{1}{b^{n}}\left(b^{d}-a^{d}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\int_{\frac{1}{b^{n}}}^{\frac{1}{a^{n}}}\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d}{np}}}dt\right)

We have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{np}=\frac{d}{\frac{2dn}{d-2}}=\frac{d-2}{2n},

and for
n\geq d-2
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{d-2}{2n}\leq\frac{1}{2}<1,

which shows that

\displaystyle\int_{\frac{1}{b^{n}}}^{\frac{1}{a^{n}}}\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d}{np}}}dt=\left[\frac{1}{1-\frac{d-2}{2n}}t^{1-\frac{d-2}{2n}}\right]_{\frac{1}{b^{n}}}^{\frac{1}{a^{n}}}=\frac{2n}{2n-(d-2)}\left(\frac{1}{a^{n-\frac{d-2}{2}}}-\frac{1}{b^{n-\frac{d-2}{2}}}\right).

Therefore, we finally get

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|x|^{n}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\frac{2n}{2n-(d-2)}\frac{1}{a^{n-\frac{d-2}{2}}}.

Therefore, we have by the elementary inequality
|Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)|\leq\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}

\displaystyle\left\|u-a_{0,1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha\,b\right)^{n+\frac{d-2}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d-2)}{2n+d-2}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{{n+\frac{d-2}{2}}}}{a^{n+\frac{d-2}{2}}}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx=\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right),

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|u-a_{0,1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{N_{d}(n)^{2}}{n}\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}N_{d}(n)^{2}\frac{4(n+d-2)}{(2n+d-2)^{2}}\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)},

which concludes the proof of the theorem.
∎

Theorem 3.12
.

Let
d\geq 3
. There exists a constant
\Gamma_{d}^{\prime}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
and assume for
d\geq 7
that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right),

and fix
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
. Then, for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have
\nabla u\in L^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})
and

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla(u-\lambda\cdot x)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

(3.56)

Proof.

Recall that by (
3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla\left(u-\sum_{k=1}^{d}a_{1,k}x_{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|r^{n-1}

\displaystyle+\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d-1)}.

In general, we see that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{l}u|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{k=0}^{l}\binom{l}{k}\Gamma_{d}(k)\right)\left(\sum_{n=l}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{l}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|r^{n-l}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(n+d-2)!}{(n+d-2-l)!}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d-2+l)}\right).

Recall also that

\displaystyle\beta(d)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

We deduce if
\lambda=\left(a_{1,1},\cdots,a_{1,d}\right)
that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(u-\lambda\cdot x\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d-2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|(\alpha\,b)^{n-1+\frac{d-2}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|b_{n,k}|\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2(n+d-1)-(d-2)}\frac{\alpha^{{n+d-1-\frac{d-2}{2}}}}{a^{n+d-1-\frac{d-2}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha\,b\right)^{n+\frac{d}{2}-2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|b_{n,k}|\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2n+d}\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2d}}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\left(\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\frac{2n+d-4}{n^{2}}N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{4(n+d-1)^{2}(n+d-2)}{(2n^{3}+1)(2n+d)}\alpha^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

∎

Theorem 3.13
.

Let
d\geq 3
. Then, there exists a universal constant
\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, define
B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
. For all
0<\alpha<1
, define
\Omega_{\alpha}=B_{\alpha\,b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\alpha^{-1}a}(0)
, and let
u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
be a harmonic function. If
d=3,4
, assume that for some
a\leq r\leq b
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\partial B(0,r)}\partial_{\nu}u\,d\mathscr{H}^{d-1}=0.

Assume that

\displaystyle\dfrac{b}{a}\geq\frac{9}{4}.

(3.57)

Then, for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\qquad&&\text{if}\;\,d=3,4\\
&\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-4}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-4}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\qquad&&\text{if}\;\,d\geq 5.\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

We have for all
n\geq 1
and for
p=\dfrac{2d}{d+2}

\displaystyle\left\||x|^{n-1}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}\leq\frac{p^{2}}{p-1}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}b^{n-1+\frac{d}{p}}=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}b^{n-1+\frac{d+2}{2}}=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}b^{n+\frac{d}{2}},

and for all
n\geq 1
, using that

\displaystyle\frac{d}{(n+d-1)p}=\frac{d}{(n+d-1)\frac{2d}{d+2}}=\frac{d+2}{2(n+d-1)}<1

for all
n\geq 0
and
d\geq 3
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{|x|^{n+d-1}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{p,1}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2(n+d-1)-(d+2)}\frac{1}{a^{n+d-1-\frac{d+2}{2}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2(n+d-1)-(d+2)}\frac{1}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}-2}},

and using (
3.2
), we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\,\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha\,b\right)^{n+\frac{d}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\Big(n+d-2+\Gamma_{d}(1)\Big)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d-1)}{2(n+d-1)-(d+2)}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}-2}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}-2}}.

Assuming for
d=3,4
that

\displaystyle\int_{\partial B(0,r)}\partial_{\nu}u\,d\mathscr{H}^{d-1}=0,

we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\left\{\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}n^{2}(2n+d)\,N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

(3.58)

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|b_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d}b^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\left(\sum_{n=\mathbf{1}_{\left\{d\leq 4\right\}}}^{\infty}\left(n+d-2\right)N_{d}(n)^{2}\frac{4(n+d-1)^{2}}{2n+d-4}\alpha^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|b_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d-4}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\qquad&&\text{if}\;\,d=3,4\\
&\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-4}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\qquad&&\text{if}\;\,d\geq 5\end{aligned}\right.

where we used that
\mathrm{deg}(n^{2}(2n+d)\,N_{d}(n)^{2})=3+2(d-2)=2d-1
and (
3.2
).
∎

Theorem 3.14
.

Let
d\geq 3
. There exists a constant
\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
and assume that
u\in W^{1,2}(\Omega)
. Then, for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have
\nabla^{2}u\in L^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})
and

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

(3.59)

Proof.

Recall the explicit upper bound:

\displaystyle|\nabla^{2}u|\leq\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|r^{n-2}

\displaystyle+\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d)}

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx=\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right).

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)\left\{\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}|a_{n,k}|\left(\alpha\,b\right)^{n-1+\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d)}{2(n+d)-(d+2)}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}-1}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}-1}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{2}(d)\right)

\displaystyle\times\left\{\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}n^{3}N_{d}(n)^{2}|a_{n,k}|\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(n+d-1)^{2}N_{d}(n)^{2}\frac{4(n+d)^{2}}{n+d-2}\alpha^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

∎

Theorem 3.15
.

Let
d\geq 3
. There exists a constant
\Gamma_{d}^{\ast\ast\ast}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
and assume for
d\geq 7
that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right),

and fix
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
. Then, for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have
\nabla^{3}u\in L^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})
and

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast\ast\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

(3.60)

Proof.

Recalling that

\displaystyle u(r,\omega)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(a_{n,k}\,r^{n}+b_{n,k}r^{-(n+d-2)}\right)Y_{n}^{k}(\omega).

In particular, we have

\displaystyle\nabla^{3}P=\nabla^{3}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{2}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}a_{n,k}r^{n}Y_{n}^{k}(\omega)\right)=0

since
P
is a is a polynomial function of order at most
2
. Recall also that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{l}_{\omega}Y_{n}^{k}|\leq\Gamma_{d}(l)n^{l}\sqrt{N_{k}(n)}

and that
\Gamma_{d}(0)=1
.
We deduce that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{3}u(r,\omega)|\leq\left|\partial_{r}^{3}\left(u-P\right)\right|+\frac{1}{r}\left|\nabla_{\omega}(\partial_{r}^{2}\left(u-P\right))\right|+\left|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\partial_{r}\left(u-P\right)\right)\right|+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\left|\nabla_{\omega}^{2}\partial_{r}\left(u-P\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\partial_{r}\left(\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\left(u-P\right)\right)\right)\right|+\frac{1}{r}\left|\nabla_{\omega}\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\left(u-P\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\nabla_{\omega}^{2}\left(u-P\right)\right)\right|+\frac{1}{r^{3}}\left|\nabla_{\omega}^{3}\left(u-P\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+3\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+3\,\Gamma_{d}(2)+\Gamma_{d}(3)\right)\left(\sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{3}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|r^{n-3}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d)(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}|r^{-(n+d+1)}\right).

Then, recall that

\displaystyle\beta(d)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

provided if
d\geq 7
that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right).

(3.61)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{2d}{d+2},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+3\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+3\,\Gamma_{d}(2)+\Gamma_{d}(3)\right)\left(\sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{3}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|b^{n-2+\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d)(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d+1)}{2(n+d+1)-(d+2)}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\left(1+3\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+3\,\Gamma_{d}(2)+\Gamma_{d}(3)\right)\left(\sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n^{3}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|b^{n-2+\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d)(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2(n+d+1)}{2n+d}|b_{n,k}|\frac{\alpha^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}{a^{n+\frac{d}{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4d^{2}}{d^{2}-4}\left(\beta(d)\right)^{\frac{d+2}{2d}}\widetilde{\Gamma_{d}}\left(\left(\sum_{n=3}^{\infty}n^{2}(2n+d-4)N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=3}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{4(n+d+1)^{2}}{2n+d}\frac{(n+d)^{2}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)}{2n^{3}+1}N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\times\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{2}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast\ast\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{\alpha^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{d}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)},

which concludes the proof of the theorem.
∎

3.3
Pointwise estimates

Theorem 3.16
.

Let
d\geq 3
. There exists a universal constant
\Lambda_{d}<\infty
with the following property. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
and assume that

\displaystyle\frac{b}{a}\geq\frac{9}{4}.

Let
u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
be a harmonic function. Assume that for
d=3,4
, we have for some
a\leq r\leq b

\displaystyle\int_{\partial B(0,r)}\partial_{\nu}u\,d\mathscr{H}^{d-1}=0.

Then,
u\in L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\Omega)
, and for all
x\in\Omega
, provided that
d=3,4
, we have

\displaystyle|u(x)|\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)},

while for
d\geq 5
, we have

\displaystyle|u(x)|\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-4}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

Proof.

Recall that by (
3.1
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|u|^{2}dx\geq\frac{\beta(d)}{8}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|a_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d}b^{2n+d}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\beta(d)}{8}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|b_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d-4}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right),

(3.62)

where
b_{0,1}
for
d=3,4
, so that the sum is well-defined for all
d\geq 3,4
, and each term in the series is positive.
We have for all
x\in\Omega

\displaystyle|u(x)|\leq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}||x|^{n}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-(n+d-2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}||x|^{n+\frac{d}{2}}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-\left(n+\frac{d}{2}-2\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+d)N_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{x}{b}\right)^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|a_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d}b^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+d-4)N_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{|b_{n,k}|^{2}}{2n+d-4}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-4}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\hskip 18.49988pt&&\text{if}\;\,d=3,4\\
&\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-4}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-4}{2}}\right)\left\|u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\hskip 18.49988pt&&\text{if}\;\,d\geq 5\end{aligned}\right.

since
\deg((2n+d)N_{d}(n)^{2})=1+2(d-2)=2d-3
. Here,
\Lambda_{d}
is explicitly given by

\displaystyle\Lambda_{d}=2\sqrt{\frac{2\,C_{d}}{\beta(d)}},

where
C_{d}<\infty
is the minimal constant such that the following inequality holds true

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+d)N_{d}(n)^{2}\beta^{n}\leq\frac{C_{d}}{(1-\beta)^{2(d-1)}}.

For example, with
d=3
, we have
N_{d}(n)=2n+1
, and we have

\displaystyle(2n+3)(2n+1)^{2}=(2n+3)(4n^{2}+4n+1)=(2n+3)(4(n-1)(n-2)+12n-7)

\displaystyle=8n(n-1)(n-2)+2n(12(n-1)+5)+3(4n(n-1)+8n+1)

\displaystyle=8n(n-1)(n-2)+24n(n-1)+10n+12n(n-1)+24n+3

\displaystyle=8n(n-1)(n-2)+36n(n-1)+10n+3

which implies as

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\beta^{n}=\frac{1}{1-\beta}

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}n\beta^{n}=\frac{\beta}{(1-\beta)^{2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}n(n-1)\beta^{n}=\frac{2\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta)^{3}}

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}n(n-1)(n-2)\beta^{n}=\frac{6\beta^{3}}{(1-\beta)^{4}}

that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+3)(2n+1)^{2}\beta^{n}=\frac{48\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta)^{4}}+\frac{72\beta}{(1-\beta)^{3}}+\frac{10\beta}{(1-\beta)^{2}}+\frac{3}{(1-\beta)}

\displaystyle=\frac{48\beta^{2}+72\beta(1-\beta)+10\beta(1-2\beta+\beta^{2})+3(1-3\beta+3\beta^{2}-\beta^{3})}{(1-\beta)^{4}}

\displaystyle=\frac{3+73\beta-35\beta^{2}+7\beta^{3}}{(1-\beta)^{4}}\leq\frac{76}{(1-\beta)^{4}},

which yields
C_{3}=76
and

\displaystyle\Lambda_{3}=2\sqrt{\frac{2C_{2}}{\beta(3)}}=2\sqrt{\frac{38}{\pi}}.

For
d=4
, we have
N_{4}(n)=(n+1)^{2}
, and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+4)(n+1)^{4}\beta^{n}=\frac{4\left(1+18\beta+33\beta^{2}+8\beta^{3}\right)}{(1-\beta)^{6}}=\frac{240}{(1-\beta)^{4}}

which yields
C_{4}=240
and

\displaystyle\Lambda_{4}=2\sqrt{\frac{C_{4}}{\beta(4)}}=\frac{2\sqrt{120}}{\pi}=\frac{8\sqrt{30}}{\pi}.

∎

Theorem 3.17
.

Let
m\geq 1
and
d\geq 3
. For all
0<a<b<\infty
, let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
, let
u:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{m}
be a harmonic map such that
\nabla u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
. Then,
\nabla u\in\mathrm{L}^{\infty}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\Omega)
and for all
x\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla u(x)|\leq\frac{\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-1}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

(3.63)

Furthermore, assuming that for some
a\leq r\leq b

\displaystyle\int_{\partial B(0,r)}\partial_{\nu}u\,d\mathscr{H}^{d-1}=0,

the following estimate holds

\displaystyle|\nabla u(x)|\leq\frac{\left(1+\Gamma_{d}(1)\right)\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

(3.64)

Proof.

Recall the identity

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx=\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right),

while

\displaystyle|\nabla u(x)|\leq\frac{1+\Gamma_{d}(1)}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}||x|^{n+\frac{d}{2}-1}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-(x+\frac{d}{2}-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\Gamma_{d}(1)}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}nN_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n|a_{n,k}|^{2}\,b^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)N_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+\Gamma_{d}(1)}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d}}}\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{2\sqrt{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-1}}}\frac{\Lambda_{d}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d-2}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

since
2n+d\geq n+d-2
. The second inequality follows immediately.
∎

Theorem 3.18
.

Let
m\geq 1
and
d\geq 3
, let
0<a<b<\infty
and let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
. If
d\geq 7
, assume that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right).

Then, for all harmonic map
u:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{m}
, if
\nabla^{2}u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
, we have
\nabla^{2}u\in L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\Omega)
and for all
x\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla^{2}u(x)|\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{d-1}\right)}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)},

where
\Lambda_{d}^{\prime}=\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{1}(d)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)(d-1)\sqrt{d-2}\,\Lambda_{d}
.

Proof.

Recall that

\displaystyle\beta(d)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{2n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)\leq\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

provided if
d\geq 7
that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{d-2}\log\left(\frac{d}{4}\right).

(3.65)

Therefeore, we get

\displaystyle|\nabla^{2}u(x)|\leq\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}n^{2}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}||x|^{n-2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-1)(n+d-2)\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-(n+d)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\left(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(2n+d-4)N_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{n^{4}}{2n+d-4}|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-4}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(2n+d)(n+d-1)^{2}(n+d-2)}{2n^{3}+1}N_{d}(n)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2n+d}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\frac{(2n^{3}+1)(n+d-2)}{2n+d}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\left(1+2\,\Gamma_{d}(1)+\Gamma_{d}(2)\right)(d-1)\sqrt{d-2}\,\Lambda_{d}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{d-2}}}\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-1}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{d-1}\right)}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

∎

Theorem 3.19
.

Let
m\geq 1
, and let
d\geq 3
. There exists a constant
\Lambda_{d}^{\prime\prime}<\infty
with the following property. Let
0<a<b<\infty
and let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
. Let
u:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{m}
be a harmonic map such that
\nabla u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
. Then, we have
u\in L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\Omega)
and there exists
c\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that for all
x\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle|u(x)-c|\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}^{\prime\prime}}{|x|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

Proof.

Recall that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla u|^{2}dx=\beta(d)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\left(n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}+(n+d-2)|b_{n,k}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right).

We have

\displaystyle|u(x)-a_{0,1}|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}||x|^{n}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-(n+d-2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|a_{n,k}|b^{n+\frac{d-2}{2}}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}\sqrt{N_{d}(n)}\,|b_{n,k}||x|^{-\left(n+\frac{d-2}{2}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|x|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}\left(\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{N_{d}(n)^{2}}{n}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}n|a_{n,k}|^{2}b^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{N_{d}(n)^{2}}{n+d-2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2n+d-2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=1}^{N_{d}(n)}(n+d-2)\frac{1}{a^{2n+d-2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}^{\prime\prime}}{|x|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

∎

Theorem 3.20
.

Let
m\geq 1
, and let
d\geq 3
. There exists a constant
\Lambda_{d}^{\prime\prime\prime}<\infty
with the following property. Let
0<a<b<\infty
and let
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{2}
. Provided that
d\geq 7
Let
u:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{m}
be a harmonic map such that
\nabla^{2}u\in L^{2}(\Omega)
. Then, we have
\nabla u\in L^{\infty}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\Omega)
and there exists
\lambda\in M_{m,d}(\mathbb{R})
such that for all
x\in\Omega
, we have

\displaystyle|\nabla\left(u(x)-\lambda\cdot x\right)|\leq\frac{\Lambda_{d}^{\prime\prime\prime}}{|x|^{\frac{d-2}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}+\frac{1}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2}\right)^{d-2}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}\right)\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}.

Proof.

The proof is identical as the previous one and we omit it.
∎

4
Energy Quantization Revisited

As we mentioned it in the introduction, the first estimate required in the proof of the Morse stability is the strong
L^{2,1}
energy quantization. We first revisit the
L^{2,1}
estimate of
[
21
]
, that is needed for the weighted estimate and to prove the strong energy quantization. We treat the case
f=0
since we will only be concerned by (intrinsic or extrinsic) biharmonic maps for which this term does not occur. However, it would be easy to add this extra term, or, as in
[
21
]
, replace
E
by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{B(0,1)}|\Delta u|^{2}dx+\int_{B(0,1)}u^{\ast}\Omega\qquad\text{or}\qquad\frac{1}{2}\int_{B(0,1)}\left|\left(\Delta u\right)^{T}\right|^{2}dx+\int_{B(0,1)}u^{\ast}\Omega,

where
\Omega
is a smooth
4
-form on
\mathbb{R}^{n}
. Recall that the energy quantization is equivalent to the
no-neck energy property,
which asserts that the energy of the
“necks”
that connect the main map to its bubbles vanishes in the limit. Explicitly, a neck region is conformally given by
\Omega_{k}(\alpha)=B_{\alpha}\setminus\overline{B}_{\alpha^{-1}\rho_{k}}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{4}
. If
\left\{u_{k}\right\}_{k\in\mathbb{N}}
is a sequence of biharmonic maps of bounded energy, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 0}\limsup_{k\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\alpha^{-1}\rho_{k}<r<\alpha\rho_{k}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u_{k}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}+\left\|\nabla u_{k}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}\right)=0.

(4.1)

For technical reasons, we will replace this condition by the following:

\displaystyle\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 0}\limsup_{k\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\alpha^{-1}\rho_{k}<r<\alpha\rho_{k}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u_{k}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u_{k}}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}\right)=0.

(4.2)

Thanks to the improved Sobolev embedding
W^{1,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})\lhook\joinrel\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow L^{4,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
, one could also characterise neck regions by the following property:

\displaystyle\lim_{\alpha\rightarrow 0}\limsup_{k\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\alpha^{-1}\rho_{k}<r<\alpha\rho_{k}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u_{k}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}+\left\|\nabla u_{k}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{r}(0))}\right)=0.

(4.3)

However, the first and third conditions are not suitable for dyadic arguments since
\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(\,\cdot\,)}^{2}
and
\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(\,\cdot\,)}^{2}
(or
\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(\,\cdot\,)}
) have bad sumability properties. Furthermore, the quantity that ones controls thanks to the Pohozaev identity (be it for the
L^{2}
energy quantization or the
L^{2,1}
energy quantization) is the one appearing in (
4.2
), which shows that this is the most adapated one to biharmonic map. Furthermore, our Whitney extension Theorem
9.13
shows that the three conditions are equivalent. Indeed, if
0<2\,a<b<\infty
,
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
, then, for all
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)
, there is an extension
\widetilde{u}\in W^{2,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
such that

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})},\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})},\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{4})},\left\|\frac{\nabla\widetilde{u}}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq 10^{5}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\min\left\{\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})},\left\|\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{4})},\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})}\right\}\right).

(4.4)

Theorem 4.1
.

Let
(M^{m},h)
be a closed Riemannian manifold isometrically embedded in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
, let
0<a<b<\infty
be such that

\displaystyle\frac{b}{a}>\frac{9}{4},

and
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)\subset\mathbb{R}^{4}
. There exists a universal constant
\varepsilon_{0}=\varepsilon_{0}(M^{m},h)>0
such that for all (intrinsic or extrinsic) biharmonic map
u\in W^{2,2}(\Omega,M^{m})
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{2a<r<\frac{b}{2}}\int_{B_{2r}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{r}{2}}(0)}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx\leq\varepsilon_{0},

there exists
N\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\displaystyle N\leq C\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx

and a subdivision
2a=a_{0}<a_{1}<\cdots<a_{N}=\dfrac{b}{2}
such that for all
0\leq i\leq N-1
, there exists
\gamma_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|A\,\nabla^{3}u-\nabla^{3}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{i}\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B_{a_{i+1}}\setminus\overline{B}_{a_{i}}(0))}+\left\|A\nabla^{2}u-\nabla^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{i}\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B_{a_{i+1}}\setminus\overline{B}_{a_{i}}(0))}

\displaystyle+\left\|A\nabla u-\nabla\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{i}\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(B_{a_{i+1}}\setminus\overline{B}_{a_{i}}(0))}\leq C\left(\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.5)

In particular, we have

\displaystyle\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla^{2}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\nabla\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx.

(4.6)

Proof.

Case 1:

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx\leq\varepsilon_{0}.

(4.7)

Thanks to our Whitney extension theorem below (Theorem
9.13
; see the inequality (
4
)), there exists an extension
\widetilde{u}\in W^{2,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
such that
\mathrm{supp}(\widetilde{u})\subset B_{2b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{a}{2}}(0)
and

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})}\leq 10^{5}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\leq 10^{5}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{0}}.

(4.8)

More precisely, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2b}\setminus\overline{B}_{b}(0))}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B_{2b}\setminus\overline{B}_{b}(0))}\leq 10^{5}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{b}{2}}(0))}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{b}{2}}(0))}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{a}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{a}{2}}(0))}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B_{a}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{a}{2}}(0))}\leq 10^{5}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2a}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0))}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B_{2a}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0))}\right).

(4.9)

Therefore, using the explicit representations given in
[
20
, Proposition
2.1
]
, we deduce that
there exists extensions

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} &\widetilde{V}\in W^{1,2}(B(0,b),\mathrm{M}_{n}(\mathbb{R})\otimes\Lambda^{1}\mathbb{R}^{4})\qquad&&\widetilde{w}\in L^{2}(B(0,b),M_{n}(\mathbb{R}))\\
&\widetilde{\omega}\in L^{2}(B(0,b),\mathfrak{so}(n))\qquad&&\widetilde{F}\in L^{2}\cdot W^{1,2}(B(0,b),\mathrm{M}_{n}(\mathbb{R})\otimes\Lambda^{1}\mathbb{R}^{4})\end{aligned}\right.

of respectively
V,w,\omega
, and
F
on
\Omega
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\widetilde{V}\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{w}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{\omega}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{F}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma\left(\left\|{V}\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|{w}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|{\omega}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|{F}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(\Omega)}\right)

for some universal constant
\Gamma<\infty
independent of
0<2\,a<b<\infty
. Then, by
[
18
, Theorem
1.5
]
, using (
4.8
), provided that
\varepsilon_{0}>0
is small enough, there exists
A\in W^{2,2}\cap L^{\infty}(B(0,b),\mathrm{GL}_{n}(\mathbb{R}))
and
B\in W^{1,(\frac{4}{3},1)}(B(0,b),\mathbb{R}^{n})
such that

\displaystyle\mathrm{dist}(A,\mathrm{SO}(n))+\left\|A\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{2,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|B\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{1,(\frac{4}{3},1)}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\widetilde{V}\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{w}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{\omega}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{F}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|{V}\right\|_{\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|{w}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|{\omega}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|{F}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}\cdot\mathrm{W}^{1,2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

(4.10)

and

\displaystyle\nabla\Delta A+(\Delta A)\,\widetilde{V}-(\nabla A)\widetilde{w}+A(\nabla\widetilde{\omega}+\widetilde{F})=\nabla^{\perp}B.

Then,
A\Delta\widetilde{u}
solves an equation of the form

\displaystyle\Delta(A\,\Delta\widetilde{u})=\dive(K)\qquad\text{in}\;\,B(0,b)

where

\displaystyle\left\|K\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,b))}\leq C\left(1+\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}.

(4.11)

Indeed,
K
is given explicitly as

\displaystyle K=2\,\nabla A\Delta\widetilde{u}-\Delta A\nabla\widetilde{u}+A\,w\nabla\widetilde{u}+\nabla A(V\nabla\widetilde{u})-A\nabla(V\nabla\widetilde{u})-B\nabla\widetilde{u}.

Then, all open
U\subset\Omega
, we have by Hölder’s inequality
L^{4,2}\cdot L^{2,2}\hookrightarrow L^{\frac{4}{3},1}

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla A\Delta\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{H}}\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\left\|\Delta\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}\leq C\left\|A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,2}(B(0,b))}\left\|\Delta\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left\|\Delta\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}.

Likewise, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\Delta A\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}\leq\left\|\Delta A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}

Notice that since
\mathrm{SO}(n)
is compact, we have by (
4
)

\displaystyle\left\|A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}(B(0,b))}\leq C\left(1+\mathrm{dist}(A,\mathrm{SO}(n))\right)\leq C\left(1+\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|A\,w\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}\leq C\left(1+\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}.

As
V\in W^{1,2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})\hookrightarrow L^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
(the improved
L^{4,2}
embedding is not needed here), we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla A(V\nabla\widetilde{u})\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\left\|V\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}\leq\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\left\|V\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(U)}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4}(U)}.

Likewise,

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla(V\nabla\widetilde{u})\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\times\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\right),

and the Sobolev embedding
W^{1,\frac{4}{3}}(\mathbb{R}^{4})\hookrightarrow L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
, we finally get

\displaystyle\left\|B\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}\leq C_{\mathrm{H}}\left\|B\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}.

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\left\|K\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(U)}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1+\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(U)}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(U)}\right).

(4.12)

Therefore, (
4.11
) follows from (
4.12
) and (
4.8
).

Now, make an expansion
A\Delta u=\varphi+\psi
, where

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \Delta\varphi&=\dive(K)\qquad&&\text{in}\;\,B(0,b)\\
\varphi&=0\qquad&&\text{on}\;\,\partial B(0,b).\end{aligned}\right.

Thanks to Calderón-Zygmund estimates and the Sobolev embedding
W^{1,(\frac{4}{3},1)}(\mathbb{R}^{4})\lhook\joinrel\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow L^{2,1}(\mathbb{R}^{4})
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\varphi\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\varphi\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,b))}\leq C\left\|K\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,b))}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

As for
d=4
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+d)N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{2n}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}(2n+4)(n+1)^{4}\alpha^{2n}=\frac{4(1+18\alpha^{2}+33\alpha^{4}+8\alpha^{6})}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{6}}\leq\frac{240}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{6}}

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{4(n+d-2)^{2}}{(2n+d-4)}N_{d}(n)^{2}\alpha^{2n+d-4}=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}4(n+2)^{2}(n+1)^{3}\frac{n+1}{2n}\alpha^{2n}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}4(n+2)^{2}(n+1)^{3}\alpha^{2n}

\displaystyle=\frac{16\alpha^{2}\left(18+22\alpha^{4}-15\alpha^{6}+6\alpha^{8}-\alpha^{10}\right)}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{6}}\leq\frac{640}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{6}}\alpha^{2},

which yields the estimate
\Gamma_{1}(4)\leq 640
and

\displaystyle C_{4}\leq 8\sqrt{2\,\Gamma_{1}(4)}=8\sqrt{2\cdot 2^{7}\cdot 5}=128\sqrt{5}.

Since
\psi
is a harmonic function, thanks to inequality (
3.37
) of Theorem
3.8
we deduce that there exists
\gamma\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
such that for all
0<\alpha<1
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\psi-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{128\sqrt{5}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{3}}\left\|\psi\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{3}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|A\Delta u-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq C\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{3}}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Then, we have

\displaystyle\dive(A\nabla\widetilde{u})=A\,\Delta\widetilde{u}+\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}\qquad\text{in}\;\,B(0,b).

Thanks to the Sobolev embedding
W^{1,2}(B(0,b))\hookrightarrow L^{4,2}(B(0,b))
and Hölder’s inequality for Lorentz spaces, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B(0,b))}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\frac{1}{b}\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}+\frac{1}{b}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}\right).

Now, we have by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality
[
2
, Exemple
1
p.
194
]

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{GN}}\left\|A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C_{\mathrm{GN}}

\displaystyle\leq(2\pi^{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}b\,C_{\mathrm{GN}}\left\|A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C\,b\,\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{GN}}\left\|\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\,b\,\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

where we used that
u
takes values into the compact manifold
M^{n}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
Therefore, we finally get the inequality

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,b))}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}.

(4.13)

Therefore, we deduce that for all
0<\alpha<1

\displaystyle\dive(A\nabla\widetilde{u})=\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}+\varphi+\left(\psi-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}=\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}+F\qquad\text{in}\;\,\Omega_{\alpha},

where

\displaystyle\left\|F\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq C\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{(1-\alpha^{2})^{3}}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Therefore, making a Hodge decomposition as in
[
21
]
(
[
17
, Corollary
10.5.1
]
)
A\,d\widetilde{u}=d\alpha+d^{\ast}\beta
, where

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \Delta\alpha&=\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}+F\qquad&&\text{in}\;\,\Omega_{\alpha}\\
\end{aligned}\right.

and

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \Delta\beta&=dA\wedge d\widetilde{u}\qquad&&\text{in}\;\,B(0,\alpha\,b)\\
\beta&=0\qquad&&\text{on}\;\,\partial B(0,\alpha\,b).\end{aligned}\right.

By standard Caldrón-Zygmund estimates and interpolation theory, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\beta\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}+\left\|\nabla\beta\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(B(0,\alpha b))}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2},

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\Delta(\nabla\beta)=\nabla dA\wedge d\widetilde{u}+dA\wedge\nabla d\widetilde{u},

and thanks to the
L^{4,2}
estimate of
\nabla A
and
\nabla\widetilde{u}
, we deduce by Hölder’s inequality for Lorentz spaces

\displaystyle\left\||\nabla^{2}A||\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,b))}+\left\||\nabla A||\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}|\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,b))}

\displaystyle\leq\Gamma_{H}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}\left\|\nabla\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B(0,b))}+\left\|\nabla A\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,2}(B(0,b))}\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{u}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(B(0,b))}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}.

Therefore, Calderón-Zygmund estimates, we also get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}\beta\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Now, recall that

\displaystyle F=\varphi+\left(\psi-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right)+\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}.

Using the estimate (
3.13
) from Theorem
3.13
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(\psi-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq\frac{\Gamma_{d}^{\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{4}}\left\|\psi-\frac{\gamma}{|x|^{2}}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}

Therefore, thanks to the previous estimates on
\varphi
and
\nabla A\cdot\nabla\widetilde{u}
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla F\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\alpha})}\leq C\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\alpha}{\left(1-\alpha^{2}\right)^{3}}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

On the other hand, let
\widetilde{F}
is a controlled extension of
F
and
\widetilde{\alpha}:B(0,\alpha\,b)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}^{n}
be such that

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} \Delta\widetilde{\alpha}&=\widetilde{F}\qquad&&\text{in}\;\,B(0,\alpha\,b)\\
\widetilde{\alpha}&=0\qquad&&\text{on}\;\,\partial B(0,\alpha\,b),\end{aligned}\right.

As previously, by Calderón-Zygmund estimates, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}\alpha\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}+\left\|\nabla^{2}\widetilde{\alpha}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}+\left\|\nabla\widetilde{\alpha}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}+\left\|\widetilde{\alpha}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\infty}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}\leq C\left\|\widetilde{F}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B(0,\alpha\,b))}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|F\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega)}.

Then, we get by the estimate (
3.46
) of Theorem
3.10
that for all
0<\beta<1

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\alpha\,\beta})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{4}^{\prime\prime}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta^{2})^{3}}\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|z|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\alpha})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta^{2})^{3}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}.

On the other hand, thanks to inequality (
3.56
) of Theorem
3.12
, there exists
c_{0}\in\mathbb{R}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)-c_{0}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\alpha\,\beta})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma_{4}^{\prime}}{\sqrt{\pi}\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta^{2})^{2}}\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\alpha})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta^{2})^{2}}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2},

but the previous
L^{4}
bound shows that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\alpha\,\beta})}\leq C\left(1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{\alpha^{2}a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{(1-\beta^{2})^{2}}\right)\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)^{2}.

Therefore, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\nabla\left(\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

In particular, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\left(\partial_{r}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\frac{1}{r^{2}}\nabla_{\omega}^{2}\alpha\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\alpha\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Finally, we have by equation (
3.60
) of Theorem
3.15
the estimate

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\alpha\,\beta})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma_{4}^{\ast\ast\ast}}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{\left(1-\beta^{2}\right)^{4}}\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(\alpha-\widetilde{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega_{\alpha})}

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha,\beta}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right),

which also gives

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{3}\left(\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\alpha\,\beta})}\leq C_{\alpha}\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\left(\partial_{r}^{2}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\partial_{r}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\frac{1}{r^{2}}\nabla_{\omega}^{2}\alpha\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\partial_{r}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}

\displaystyle+\left\|\partial_{r}\left(\frac{1}{r^{2}}\nabla_{\omega}^{2}\alpha\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\frac{1}{r^{3}}\nabla_{\omega}^{3}\alpha\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega)}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Thanks to the Hodge decomposition
A\,d\widetilde{u}=d\alpha+d^{\ast}\beta
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla^{2}\left(A\nabla u-\nabla\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|\nabla\left(A\nabla u-\nabla\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}

\displaystyle+\left\|A\nabla u-\nabla\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

(4.14)

Furthermore, the previous
L^{4,2}
estimate (
4.13
) shows that

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla^{2}u-\nabla^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

(4.15)

Likewise, as
\nabla^{2}A\cdot\nabla u\in L^{2}\cdot L^{4,2}\hookrightarrow L^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})
by Hölder’s inequality, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla^{3}u-\nabla^{3}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right).

Since
\log|x|
only appears in purely radial derivatives, we deduce the following estimates

\displaystyle\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{4,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right)

\displaystyle\left\|A\nabla^{2}\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\nabla\left(\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla u\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}+\left\|A\frac{1}{r}\nabla_{\omega}\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{\frac{4}{3},1}(\Omega_{\frac{1}{4}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left\|\nabla^{2}u\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}+\left\|\frac{\nabla u}{|x|}\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2}(\Omega)}\right),

which concludes the proof of the theorem in the first case.

Case 2: General Case
.
Thanks to the non-concentration hypothesis in all dyadic annuli, we can follow the argument of
[
19
]
to find a sequence of radii
4a=4a_{0}<a_{1}=b_{0}<\cdots a_{i+1}=b_{i}<\cdots b_{N}=4b
, where

\displaystyle\int_{B_{4b_{i}}\setminus\overline{B}_{\frac{a_{i}}{4}}(0)}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx\leq\varepsilon(M^{m},h)\qquad\text{and}\;\,N\leq\frac{1}{\varepsilon(M^{m},h)}\int_{\Omega}\left(|\nabla^{2}u|+\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\right)dx.

Therefore, applying the previous step, we deduce that for all
0\leq i\leq N-1
, there exists
\gamma_{i}\in\mathbb{R}^{n}
such that

\displaystyle\left\|A\,\nabla^{3}u-\nabla^{3}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{i}\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B_{a_{i+1}}\setminus\overline{B}_{a_{i}}(0))}+\left\|A\nabla^{2}u-\nabla^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}\gamma_{i}\log|x|\right)\right\|_{\mathrm{L}^{2,1}(B_{a_{i+1}}\setminus\overline{B}_{a_{i}}(0))}
